## Playwright e2e — editor feature matrix (`e2e/editor/`)

`floating-toolbar.spec.ts` checks that selection and link actions move with their text synchronously during scrolling, before Floating UI or React position updates can run; top-toolbar formatting keeps the selection toolbar mounted and open, while collapsed selections still dismiss it.

MSW + Vite only (`pnpm e2e:msw:editor`); no Docker. CI runs it in two shards
(`e2e_editor`, `--shard=N/2`) in parallel with the real-stack job. The editor project uses
full Chromium's headless mode because the separate headless shell can strand
modified-click tabs ([Playwright #42142](https://github.com/microsoft/playwright/issues/42142)).

CI uploads both browser suites' reports and failure traces for seven days,
including failed attempts whose retry succeeds.
The Office save-recovery scenarios allow two minutes overall for native
initialization and a full Vite reload; their individual assertion deadlines
remain unchanged.

| File                                                                                | About                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [`e2e/editor/workspace-phone.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/workspace-phone.spec.ts) | The workspace at 360px: the header keeps title, view/edit and ⋮ (no mark-as-read book), ⋮ holds Add file, Add chapter, Mark as read and Workspace settings, the title opens the Files dropdown with a plain workspace row and pinned Workspace settings, and a file pick closes it; the chat button opens a focused field that folds back empty, keeps a draft through a blur, folds into the button on a scroll either way (the draft returns with a tap), send grows it into the chat page, and Back leaves a chip that reopens the conversation or closes. |
| [`e2e/editor/file-tree.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/file-tree.spec.ts) | Native workspace tree drags while a note editor is mounted: chapter ordering, collapsed-chapter moves, mixed file/material ordering, outer-tree unfiling, drop indicators, row-menu suppression during a drag and its return after Escape cancels it, persisted placement, and the floating Add menu sharing header actions without opening-click selection or outside-click file activation, with keyboard dismissal. |
| [`e2e/editor/block-interactions.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/block-interactions.spec.ts) | Context menu duplicate/delete/turn-into and drag reorder verified by document text order; chunked-note gutter hit testing and actual drag reordering with content-visibility retained; todo checkbox toggling; an emptied todo keeping its placeholder and taking typing; horizontal-rule padding selection, visible selection ring, Backspace/Delete removal and undo; shared callout/code style popovers updating blocks and restoring editor focus; column drag reorder and no column handles in View. |
| [`e2e/editor/formatting.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/formatting.spec.ts)                 | Block-type popover keyboard selection, current-choice check and restored editor focus; bold apply/clear and stacked marks; shared top/floating pressed state, selection preservation, caret-driven marks/list/table/column states, repeated column layout changes without blank-line accumulation, alignment selection, link active state and floating edit/remove actions through the shared Input fields, narrow-screen scrolling with settings-controlled group visibility, and Yjs Undo/Redo availability and execution after the editor loses selection. |
| [`e2e/editor/insertions.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/insertions.spec.ts)                 | Mentions in heading/paragraph, slash groups named by their visible headings (the media group offering image, YouTube and audio only) with keyboard wrap/scroll/filter and table insertion, keyboard table insertion through nested popovers with editor focus restoration, and table of contents following a retitle. |
| [`e2e/editor/note-assets.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/note-assets.spec.ts) | Deleting a note image and undoing brings it back (the server trash keeps it; nothing is stored in the browser). |
| [`e2e/editor/upload-placeholder.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/upload-placeholder.spec.ts) | With the upload's completion held, an image pasted as a file, as a file with its `<img>` (Copy image), as HTML, as markdown, dropped, or picked from the toolbar is one skeleton placeholder (no image, no "Add an image") until the asset id returns, then the image alone, after exactly one reservation (Epo 2026-10-10); a refused upload (full storage) leaves the empty placeholder and shows the storage notice; a pasted 5 MiB PDF gets a placeholder that refuses it with the image type message, and nothing is reserved (Epo 2026-10-15). Upload presence, with a headless second editor in the note's mock room (`mockNotePeer`): its upload is a skeleton here (no "Add an image"), its landed image replaces it, and a second upload goes back to "Add an image" when that editor leaves; this tab's pasted file is named in the presence the peer sees until the asset id returns. A DOCX with two pictures imports as its text plus two skeletons that become images (Epo 2026-10-10, main 1be046ea). Screenshots of each state go to the test's output folder. |
| [`e2e/editor/embed-insert.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/embed-insert.spec.ts) | With the slash menu's quiz create request held, the user clicks another paragraph, presses Enter and types; once released the quiz lands right after the line the command ran on and the typed text stays whole in the user's new line. When the caret stayed, the quiz takes the command's line and the caret (the slash input's re-select and focus leave the caret where the command ran). |
| [`e2e/editor/import-placement.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/import-placement.spec.ts) | The toolbar's Import Markdown with the caret inside a line splits the line there with the document between the halves, and the caret follows the document. |
| [`e2e/editor/paste.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/paste.spec.ts) | One paragraph per copied `<div>` line, and markdown copied from VS Code renders as a heading, inline code and a Mermaid block instead of a code block; a quiz block pasted from another note opens the note's own copy in the block's quiz editor and one whose quiz is gone is removed, and the same quiz pasted twice becomes two copies (two distinct editor reads, neither the original). A note's own quiz block pasted into it again becomes a second block that edits a second copy. Blocks pasted onto a clicked quiz block go in right after it, each with its own copy. A pasted `<img>` the browser can read (a `data:` URL) uploads through the editor's asset path and lands between the paragraphs it sat between, named `pasted-image.png`. Markdown pasted with a quiz fence creates the quiz first (one embedded create carrying its question) and the block then shows it, the text before it in place (live-items REVIEW5 pending references). |
| [`e2e/editor/editor-settings.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/editor-settings.spec.ts) | General/Commands tabs, cancelled width drafts, the applied Full width setting persisting across reload, and pane-wide Slate margin focus and keyboard undo. |
| [`e2e/editor/document-pages.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/document-pages.spec.ts) | SPA links across workspace rows, the Files page's Blocks and Files tabs, and dashboard recents; per-item cached modes, fresh View defaults, explicit URL priority and modified-click new tabs; View/Edit toggling by mouse and keyboard, mode URLs and reloads for materials/PDFs with citation-page retention, a chat citation opening the three-page scenario PDF, scrolling its box to the middle of the viewer and flashing it only after the page painted and the scroll settled, the flash ending after 1.6 s, the box over the cited line, and a second click from the top scrolling and flashing again, with mode controls inside the shared header across file/material switches and reloads, and distinct note/Markdown icons; PDF pen/text/shape/color controls, cleared-selection highlighting, precise pen erasing, undo/redo ID restoration, durable private marks with session-only history, Draw/Shape popovers and active tool states, and responsive toolbar scrolling with unclipped popovers. |

| [`e2e/editor/ui-motion.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/ui-motion.spec.ts) | Behaviour around popup and menu exits (the timing and geometry checks were removed on 2026-10-06): a closing floating toolbar is inert and survives a rapid reopen; create/edit workspace tag autocomplete with wheel scrolling inside modal dialogs, selection, focus, Escape/Tab/outside-click dismissal and closing settings after a successful save; single-execution keyboard menus with late events on retained closed content, valid trigger reopening and submenu refocus; reduced motion removing closed popups and replaced copy at once; All blocks and toolbar popover triggers toggling by click and keyboard; command palette search focus over rapid reopens; icon chooser browsing and draft confirmation; fresh workspace forms and stable previewed default-icon persistence; feature-gated AI input refocus runs with `VITE_FEATURE_EDITOR_AI=true`; fixture in `e2e/fixtures/ui-motion.*`. |
| [`e2e/editor/react-selection-walk.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/react-selection-walk.spec.ts) | A keystroke's commit reads the note editor root's `contentEditable` property (answered `inherit`, so React skips its selection walk) while the attribute stays editable; fails if a React upgrade stops consulting the property. |
| [`e2e/editor/html-embed-frame.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/html-embed-frame.spec.ts) | The feature matrix note's interactive block (a `width:100%` range input) fits its frame with nothing to scroll either way, in the real frame page `vite-embed.ts` serves; it failed with the snippet 4 px wider than the frame before `embed/index.html` zeroed range inputs' side margins (checked 2026-10-12). |
| [`e2e/editor/share-page-policy.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/share-page-policy.spec.ts) | Under the enforced script policy (`script-src` with no `'unsafe-inline'` or `'unsafe-eval'`), the MSW shared note renders its formulas as static markup and hydrates its islands (the Mermaid diagram draws, the embedded quiz's formula runs MathLive, the interactive block's frame from `VITE_EMBED_ORIGIN` runs its snippet), the shared quiz hydrates with its formulas, the shared flashcard set hydrates, and the workspace summary's page script runs after its post-paint imports, each with no enforced `securitypolicyviolation`. Fails without the template hashes (the dev server's refresh preamble is blocked) or without `src/lib/zodJitless.ts` first in `src/share/main.tsx` (Zod's `new Function` probe is blocked). Run `pnpm run e2e:msw:editor --workers=1 share-page-policy.spec.ts`. |
| [`e2e/editor/boot-failure.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/boot-failure.spec.ts) | A page whose entry script 404s (the deploy window's fresh load, Epo 2026-10-17) replaces the boot spinner with the reload message from `index.html`: the `error_chunk_title` heading and `error_chunk_body` in an alert, in English for `en-US` and Chinese for `zh-CN`, and its Reload loads the page again. A 404 same-origin stylesheet (one the test adds to the dev page, which links none) shows the message too, and the entry then never mounts React over it (Epo 2026-10-17). Fails without `index.html`'s boot script, its stylesheet case or `src/main.tsx`'s check for the message, or with its strings out of step with `messages/`. Run `pnpm run e2e:msw:editor --workers=1 boot-failure.spec.ts`. |
| [`e2e/editor/unsupported-block.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/unsupported-block.spec.ts) | A note holding a block type the embed catalog does not list opens read-only (decision 5, Epo 2026-10-13): Edit hears `room-read-only` with `code: unsupported_block` from the room (the MSW room as the collaboration server sends it) and drops to View under the strip saying why, with no editable surface; View draws the note and the block's text with the unsupported notice under it. |
| [`e2e/editor/view-mode.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/view-mode.spec.ts) | Edit to View in one tab shows the just-typed text at once while the material refetch is held, then the projected copy replaces it with no loader or missing frame. A note opened straight in View keeps its document through re-renders of the pane once the renderer has loaded: the interactive frame does not reload and the embedded quiz keeps its answer. A room turned read-only (its unsaved edits discarded) falls back to View with the cached copy, never the editor's value. |
| [`e2e/editor/thumbnail-capture.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/thumbnail-capture.spec.ts) | A PPTX edit in the editor is drawn after 5 s idle and the page hiding sends exactly one `POST /api/files/bio-office-pptx/thumbnail`; hiding again sends nothing more; a later edit is sent when the editor closes for View (fails without the upload on leaving, checked 2026-10-11). A DOCX edit (`exchange-plan.docx`) is drawn from page 1 and the page hiding sends exactly one `POST /api/files/bio-office-docx/thumbnail`. An XLSX first-sheet edit followed at once by a click on the second sheet's tab is drawn on the way and sent once, and a later edit on the second sheet neither starts a capture nor drops that drawing (fails with either the first-sheet rule or the tab-click drawing reverted, checked 2026-10-11). Opening a PPTX the mock marks stale (`capy.mock.thumbnailStale.<id>` in sessionStorage, cleared by a capture) for editing sends exactly one capture with no edit, and neither the page hiding, leaving for View nor the next open sends another. |

The narrow-toolbar checks in [`formatting.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/formatting.spec.ts) and [`document-pages.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/document-pages.spec.ts) exercise the shared `useHorizontalWheelScroll` hook with real wheel input in both directions for Plate and PDF, including PDF remounting through client navigation. The Plate check hovers a tooltip before scrolling its button offscreen and samples frames to check that the page gains no horizontal overflow.

The UI motion suite also checks the shared icon chooser's style jumps and scroll tracking, cancelled drafts, mobile confirmation, reopening at the saved selection, and keyboard dismissal.

The [manual dialog rendering check](frontend/motion.md) covers final-frame pixel
stability in the icon picker, workspace edit/create and task edit dialogs.

Manual input-error motion check (2026-09-14): real Input, underline Input,
Textarea and TagSelect at its tag limit each ran one 280ms shake and message
fade; typing/copy updates did not replay, valid-to-invalid reset them, border
sizes stayed constant, and reduced motion disabled both animations while
keeping messages visible. This used a temporary component preview, not an
automated test. Behavior is documented in [frontend motion](frontend/motion.md).

## Playwright perf (`bench/editor/`)

MSW + Vite (`pnpm bench:editor`). 6 cases total: 4 budget specs always run; 2 diagnostic V8 profiles are skipped unless `PERF_PROFILE=1`. Budgets are regression tripwires under CPU throttle, not UX targets. How to run the suite, what the GHA snapshot compare does, and why deltas stay warn-only: [editor-perf.md](editor-perf.md).

| File                                                                        | About                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [`bench/editor/scripts/editor.perf.ts`](../bench/editor/scripts/editor.perf.ts)                     | 4 cases: open cost (near-limit), typing latency (small; p95 key event and blocking per keystroke gated, worst keystroke a warning), typing + save cycle (near-limit; same typing gate), scroll FPS (near-limit). |
| [`bench/editor/scripts/saveCycleProfile.perf.ts`](../bench/editor/scripts/saveCycleProfile.perf.ts) | 1 case, opt-in (`PERF_PROFILE=1`): V8 CPU profile of the near-limit save cycle (diagnostic, no budget assert).                                          |
| [`bench/editor/scripts/typingProfile.perf.ts`](../bench/editor/scripts/typingProfile.perf.ts)       | 1 case, opt-in (`PERF_PROFILE=1`): V8 CPU profile of idle, heading typing, and body typing with per-suspect attribution (diagnostic, no budget assert). |

Office runtime (`pnpm bench:office`, production build, [editor-perf.md](editor-perf.md#office-runtime-pnpm-benchoffice)):

| File                                                                    | About                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [`bench/editor/scripts/runtime.office.ts`](../bench/editor/scripts/runtime.office.ts) | 26 cases. Per file (small and large DOCX, XLSX and PPTX): open to first paint, View to Edit ready and its first painted frame (report-only for XLSX/PPTX), keystroke to painted frame at the start and (report-only) at the end of the file, and heap after each: JS, the frames' WASM per module, each worker's JS and WASM (the DOCX engine); a co-editor case (ten remote edits from the mock room as a second peer: queue, in-frame synchronous apply (DOCX: hand-off only), receipt to paint or next frame, long tasks; report-only); worker memory only after the budgeted steps; then heap over two full view-mode passes; for the small files and the long DOCX, heap after each of five view-mode closes. XLSX also: a peer's 8,000-cell paste and row insert and the same done locally (long tasks and delay to the painted grid), typing twenty values while a peer pastes and inserts a row (every value lands, the selection walks on), and scrolling a 50,000-row sheet (paints and frames per second); report-only. Every result records the runner's CPU. Fails on unpainted keys, typing that sends no edit (at either end), a remote edit that never arrives or paints or a runtime error, a viewer or editor `ready` without timings, a worker fallback or a missed budget (calibrated on CI, 30 ms keystroke floor); heap is report-only. Runs in the `Performance` workflow's `office` job (dispatch only). |

Collaboration stress (`pnpm bench:stress`, Docker, [editor-perf.md](editor-perf.md#collaboration-stress-pnpm-benchstress)):

| File | About |
| ---- | ----- |
| [`bench/collaboration/scripts/stress.ts`](../bench/collaboration/scripts/stress.ts) | 20 peers per room type markers into one Office (`exchange-plan.docx`, uploaded through the reserve, presigned PUT and complete flow) and one Plate room for 3 minutes with offline drops and rejoins, against the e2e stack plus a fake S3; then 5 peers per room in the ~2 MB load-test note and a 4 MiB text source (report-only latency); then one room kind at a time for the server's memory per room, CPU per update and CPU per explicit save (report-only), with the server's container CPU and memory (collaboration, API, database) and, outside the budgeted phase, an event-loop-only endpoint's answer time measured from outside per phase; report-only steps that cannot finish are listed apart and fail nothing. Fails on non-convergence, a missing or doubled marker or a collaboration error in any room, exit 2 on a missed first-phase p95 budget (calibrated on CI). Runs in the `Performance` workflow's `stress` job (dispatch only). |

Shared pages on UAT (`pnpm bench:share-pages`, the `Performance` workflow's `share_pages` job, [bench/share-pages/README.md](../bench/share-pages/README.md)):

| File | About |
| ---- | ----- |
| [`bench/share-pages/scripts/share-pages.ts`](../bench/share-pages/scripts/share-pages.ts) | Creates a link-shared workspace (store-only files), quiz, flashcard set (its cards written through the set's live room) and every-block note on UAT through the API, plus a copy of the note without Mermaid and HTML blocks and the near-limit 2 MB load-test note (streamed by the Worker), then per page: fails unless the page with only its inlined CSS has the same computed styles and screenshots as with the full stylesheets (light and dark, phone and desktop width), a render is `SHARED_CACHE` with no Set-Cookie or Vary, another visitor (cookies, language, user agent) and HEAD get the same bytes as a Workers Cache `HIT`, and a forged link is an uncached `no-store` 404; measures Lighthouse desktop (applied throttling, uncached and cached) and mobile (simulated, cached only) TTFB, FCP, LCP, CLS with the shifting elements, TBT and bytes by type, uncached (unique query, Worker render, each run's CF-Cache-Status checked; only runs a warm Worker isolate rendered count, up to twenty tries, and new-isolate runs are reported apart) and cached, median of five, with the Worker's Server-Timing (API read, total) and each median run's DevTools trace, and fails when a median goes over its budget (`BUDGETS`). Deletes what it created. |

[`bench/editor/scripts/snapshot.ts`](../bench/editor/scripts/snapshot.ts) typed assembly/comparison,
[`bench/editor/scripts/compare-cli.ts`](../bench/editor/scripts/compare-cli.ts) workflow adapter, and

## Manual / diagnostic (not in CI suites)

Query freshness check: notifications and workspace chapter, file, and material
lists use a five-second stale time with focus refetching; other queries inherit
the five-minute default unless they declare an override. Workspace list route
prefetches use the same five-second options as their hooks.
File and material details also use five seconds: reopen a cached item after
five seconds to check its refresh, and verify tab focus alone does not refetch it.

Workspace-list cache check: in MSW, reload `/workspaces` and verify one
`GET /api/workspaces?sort=accessed` request. The route loader and page use
`{ sort: 'accessed', tag: [] }` to share the prefetched cache entry.

Workspace source-preview check: in MSW Biology 101, click `Biology notes - pending.md`
and `Biology notes - processing.md` in the file list. Both should open their
Markdown content while retaining the processing progress banner. These fixtures
have uploaded bytes but are not indexed yet.

| File                                                                                                | About                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [`knowledge_base_pilot.py`](../bench/rag/scripts/knowledge_base_pilot.py), [`knowledge_base_realtime.py`](../bench/rag/scripts/knowledge_base_realtime.py), [`knowledge_base_batch.py`](../bench/rag/scripts/knowledge_base_batch.py), [`test_knowledge_base_pilot.py`](../bench/rag/scripts/test_knowledge_base_pilot.py) | Local three-book parsing, embeddings and isolated pgvector pilot. Model stages run through `--provider ollama` (GLM-5.3-Flash on the local Ollama cloud model) or `tokenhub` with a 429 backoff; Qwen normal/Batch receipts remain readable. Tests cover provider dispatch, endpoint-switching retries, `run_topics`, optional `proposed_topic` and accepted tag failures of any kind. The summaries stage is deleted; `tag_outputs`/`tags_document` are the shared `tags.json` writer the builder's tag stage imports, and `evidence_verified` is the shared tolerant verifier (case, whitespace, ellipsis with every piece verbatim) both use. Frozen 24-query A/B comparison adds role/topic tags to hybrid candidates. Offline tests cover immutable input/reuse identity, persisted uncertain calls, explicit retry accounting, schema rejection, HTTP error receipts, driver ordering, source identity, figure geometry and material provenance. Figure tests check that image blocks 2 units thin are not figure records. They also check that on a PyMuPDF-built page a circle with angled lines becomes one `vector_drawing` record, with its section path, id and excerpt link, while a text callout box, a running-head rule and a fraction set as filled glyph outlines do not. A short `Figure N:` line under a centred diagram merges into its `vector_drawing` record (caption, caption box, block index, section path, the excluded flag its caption box names, and the excerpt link through the caption) with no `caption_page_reference` left, while a caption too far below keeps its own record. Excludes production queues, B2, authorization and app persistence. |
| [`lab/knowledge/tests/download-filter.cjs`](../lab/knowledge/tests/download-filter.cjs) | Browser check for download error filters: posts manual NonCommercial rejections and removes them from the 403 filter, hides the action for downloaded and no-licence items, groups HTTP statuses across URLs and duplicate PDFs across hashes while retaining record details, filters rejection reasons and no-error rows, preserves selection across refresh, and shows an empty result when matches disappear. Run `node lab/knowledge/tests/download-filter.cjs`. |
| [`lab/knowledge/tests/`](../lab/knowledge/tests/) | Knowledge-base builder: store queue operations, concurrent URL claims with host-delay reservations and restart recovery, sha256 dedupe, per-host delay across page fetches and downloads, review tasks and the startup reset; manual NonCommercial rejection API preserves licence evidence and refuses non-403 and active/downloaded items; the licence gate table including US jurisdiction suffixes, NC/ND exclusions and GNU aliases, worker failure cause preservation, and irrelevant rejection before licence checks, subject schema accepting parent subjects and rejecting invented IDs; catalog-only discovery and individual-book metadata gating, authorless downloads and reviewed authorless admission/manifest/publication checks, Free Courseware title/filename rejection including queued downloads, source-bound PDF/follow links and licence quotes, preserving anchor text/ARIA labels/title/image alt and merging duplicate URL labels, direct PDFs refusing inherited metadata, HTML content negotiation, mixed HTTP/HTTPS links and same-catalog pagination preserving depth; URL normalisation with depth and host caps; download hashing, duplicates, retries, robots, the 200 MB cap and corrupt PDFs against a local `http.server`; topic merge by id, label and alias with the 64 stop, subject-id renames and the shared-prefix TOC; the transcribe stage (the alignment table: exact, dropped numbers restored, coverage below 0.9 held, ratio held, short fragment skipped; the tight-gap tail extension; page-spanning chunks; escaped newlines unescaped outside LaTeX commands; figure matching by label then order; descriptions on the excerpt's first chunk `indexed_text` only; the live path aligning, holding, skipping and describing; accept and reject with an empty span refused; a page failing both live passes failing the stage; batch submit/wait/collect/live-retry with a fake client, thinking off in the batch body, `review_tasks` per stage, idempotent re-runs, `--live` refused while a batch is open, `--redo`; `poll` recording a missing key instead of raising); the tag stage (the tolerant verifier table, eight per request with a missing excerpt re-sent singly, `tags.json` equal to `apply_tags` on the same outputs, the 2% ceiling, the candidate prefix and evidence rule, batch collect then singles); the ingest runner (pause, failure, retry, dependency refusal pausing the workflow, waiting on a batch at transcribe and tag and resuming on a terminal poll, publish refusing undecided held chunks, `books.json` export); `llm` wait-and-retry on connection error, 429 and 5xx with a Retry-After HTTP date falling back to the pause, one schema retry, the Alibaba strict body; `slug()` collisions and the manual gate (optional edition, evidence page). Run with `uv run --project pipeline --extra test pytest lab/knowledge/tests -q`; `python lab/knowledge/server.py --check` is the self-check. |
| [`bench/rag/curated/`](../bench/rag/curated/) | Fictional 23-document RAG corpus with 48 pre-labelled questions and a missing-register control workspace; real upload/parse/ingest, source-span verification, full search/read evidence, resumable sequential agent comparisons, and isolated retrieval/prompt variants. `python bench/rag/curated/scripts/check_grading.py` verifies required hops and numeric/decimal fidelity. Generated sources and raw runs stay on the ingest VM. |
| [`bench/rag/broad/`](../bench/rag/broad/) | Frozen public-data RAG evaluation: seven native MIRACL language pools, full BEIR SciFact/ArguAna corpora, 360 hybrid/dense/exact retrieval queries, and 36 native/cross-language/multi-document/missing-source chat cases under two conditions and two repeats. Component indexes are separate from normal Markdown QA uploads. `run_retrieval.self_check()` verifies passage-position metrics, duplicate chunks, aliases, and misses; chat scoring requires source checks and explicit answer review. Raw data stays in the isolated VM lab. |
| [`bench/rag/embedding/`](../bench/rag/embedding/) | Five embedding conditions over the frozen 360-query corpus, exact hybrid/dense relevance, separate filtered HNSW overlap, storage/build cost, 1,080 uncached latency requests, and baseline/challenger agent comparison on 48 curated cases twice. `embed.self_check()` checks response shape and cache role separation; source fingerprints guard reuse. Raw attempts, sources and semantic reviews are archived locally before removing experiment resources from the VM. |
| [`bench/rag/qwen38/`](../bench/rag/qwen38/) | Qwen3.8 Flash through Alibaba Cloud with the same 48 curated questions, two repeats and Qwen4/Voyage embeddings. Reuses frozen document vectors and agent code; `qwen38.self_check()` guards the thinking-mode translation, live pilots verify streaming tool calls, `compare.py` joins all source-reviewed answers to the prior DeepSeek run with question-cluster intervals, and `probe.py` separately replays empty final responses with explicit tool disabling. Results and the audited archive/cleanup scope are in `reports/2026-09-06-qwen38.md`. |
| [`bench/parsers/scripts/budget.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/budget.py) | Parser performance budget over the fixtures in `bench/parsers/fixtures/budget/budget.json` (inside the upload limits: 2 to 1,400 pages, up to 25.7 MiB, 120 OCR pages, DOCX, XLSX, PPTX): execution time, per-stage seconds, peak memory, page and "OCR" counts (the text-less pages rendered for the ingest worker's VLM), content size and the SHA-256 of `content_list.json` per fixture, through the parser image in CI (`.github/workflows/parser-budget.yml`: parser pushes, dispatch, and Deploy ingest before a production release) or in-process locally; `check` fails on a crash, a broken invariant or an exceeded budget, `propose` and `compare` derive budgets and revision deltas, `compare` also saying per fixture whether the output is the same. |

## Site Worker and deployment configuration

UAT's scriptless API route is an edge configuration check, separate from Worker
unit tests. The [deployment runbook](deployment-runbook.md#101-uat-api-routing-without-workers)
describes API/authenticated SSE probes, preserved cache/security headers, summary
and asset checks, and a Worker tail proving `/api/*` bypasses execution.

Run `pnpm test:site`. |
| `workers/site/public.test.ts` | Share pages and their images: forged, unsigned and old JSON paths never reach the API; the page templates (`/share.html`, `/summary.html`, `/summary`) answer the 404 page and every environment routes them to the Worker; a shared page renders from the API read with its byline, inlined stylesheet and hydration state, a private item gets the uncached 404 page; a long note streams, its first blocks after the head's rules and a later slice's new rules before that slice; quiz and flashcard images pass only as raster types; a shared note's audio (GET and HEAD) answers `302` to Go's link with `no-store` and no body after one API read, its images still proxy with the shared cache, SVG and PDF answer 503, and audio on a quiz route is refused (Epo 2026-10-15); a shared page carries the image policy report-only. | `vendor/betteroffice/packages/pptx-react/src/clipboard.test.ts` | PPTX copy serialisation: plain text and escaped HTML across runs and paragraphs (bold, italic, underline set on the run), a whole story without a trailing newline, soft breaks, the empty paragraph a selection ends in, and a shape's stories in tree order with hidden and blank ones skipped. Run with the fork's `bun test --isolate packages/pptx-react`. |
| `vendor/betteroffice/packages/pptx-react/src/PptxEditor.test.tsx` | PPTX copy in edit and read-only mode: a shape click and a double-clicked word copy the right text; read-only leaves the deck unchanged on typing, paste, cut, Backspace, Delete and Enter; a read-only editor keeps its toolbar row with every control disabled but the overflow menu. `onFirstPaint` fires once per opened deck, when the first slide's paint settles, with the deck the editor holds. |
| `vendor/betteroffice/packages/pptx/src/wasm/paragraphRange.test.ts` | `replaceText` across two paragraphs (delete, type, paste with and without newlines, Enter) keeps the first paragraph's id and every resulting paragraph keeps its alignment; one undo restores the story exactly and the edit survives save and reopen; a refused replacement changes nothing and saving still works; `deleteText` still refuses the range. |
| `vendor/betteroffice/packages/pptx/src/wasm/paragraphSplit.test.ts` | Splitting a paragraph keeps its id on the first half and all its properties (spacing and indent included) on both halves, in the snapshot, the layout and the saved file; Enter then Backspace restores the paragraph and the saved slide XML exactly; undo and redo are one step; a peer's concurrent typing lands in the second half; concurrent splits of one paragraph converge and both copies lay out and save with its properties. |
| `vendor/betteroffice/packages/pptx-react/src/PptxEditor.paragraphRange.test.tsx` | Typing, paste, multi-line paste, Backspace, Delete, Enter and IME composition over a cross-paragraph selection each undo with one Ctrl+Z; a refused paste reports an error without blocking `flushPendingInput` or save; Enter then Backspace restores the paragraph and the saved slide exactly. |
| `vendor/betteroffice/packages/pptx-react/src/SlideThumbnail.test.tsx` | A strip thumbnail that re-renders while an earlier paint still awaits its image shows exactly one fresh paint of the slide (the stale paint once drew unscaled over the resized canvas). |

| `vendor/betteroffice/packages/pptx-react/src/notesWindow.test.ts` | The notes window store hands a window to subscribers, forgets it when the user closes it (a late close of an older window changes nothing), closes it through its host; a page's own pop-up opens with Google Slides' presenter size and reports a blocked one. |

Run with the fork's `bun test --isolate packages/docx-react`. |
| `src/office-runtime/viewToggles.test.ts` | The remembered Office view toggles (PPTX speaker notes, DOCX rulers) start off, each remembers its last choice for the next open without touching the other, and blocked storage starts them off without throwing; Presenter view's notes size is unset until picked, then the last pick. |
| `src/office-runtime/notesWindow.test.ts` | The PPTX presenter window Capy opens: the runtime takes it only for the token it expects while it expects one (not on a reload, not after the show ended), closes it by message, also when the frame's `pagehide` comes or its viewer or editor unmounts (then a newer one's hook stays), and asks Capy for one; the window hands itself to the runtime frame among Capy's frames (other origins throwing), then drops its opener, closes on the runtime's message only from its own origin, closes once its frame is gone (`closed`), and closes when nobody expects it. (security regression: pptx-presenter REVIEW-1 L3) |
| `src/office-runtime/pptxMenus.test.ts` | PPTX header menus pass the host's menu validation in English and Chinese with the Present action; Present is a split button whose items (From this slide and From the start handing over full screen, Presenter view opening the presenter window, in Chinese too) are also View › Present, disabled in an editor without slides (the split action's items too); edit items say whether they edit from pptx-react's `PPTX_COMMAND_EDITS` (only Capy's outputs, Select all, the three ways to present, zoom and speaker notes do not); edit items take disabled and ticked states from the editor's command state (View › Show speaker notes included), Insert › Image is a pick item; Edit holds Select all, Format the strikethrough to subscript, size, vertical alignment, indent, line spacing (ticked), add/remove space and clear formatting items and the list styles ticked only for the selection's list kind, Arrange the Align, Distribute and Center on page values; view mode lists only Download, PNG, Print, the three ways to present, View › Zoom (Fit and 50–200%, the same items as edit mode's, ticked from the zoom, none editing) and Show speaker notes, ticked from the viewer's notes state; a menu id carries its command value after the first colon. |

BetterOffice's native structural CRDT and headless adapter tests live in the
vendored fork. Its DOCX `seedFixtures.parity.test.ts` also covers concurrent
threads/replies/resolve, native and projected seeds agreeing on every comment range (the `comment-ranges` fixture) and on a simple field inside a tracked insertion (the `tracked-changes` fixture), preserved anchors, OOXML export/reopen and stable numeric
comment-ID mapping. Generated WASM must match those fork sources before testing.

The vendored `packages/xlsx-react/src/XlsxEditor.test.tsx` also checks host-triggered flush of open cell and formula drafts before checkpoint/export, without relying on blur, and that a read-only editor keeps its toolbar with only Search menus, the zoom box (zoom edits nothing), the overflow menu and PNG export live, and its formula bar read-only rather than disabled.

Independent Ops deployment checks in `scripts/env/test_config.py` cover scoped
configuration rendering, excluded application secrets and auth bypasses, required
Ops settings, environment matching, and refusal to target the main Coolify stack
or applications without a shared destination and predefined networking.
Run `pnpm test:deployment`.

### Native OpenDataLoader accuracy experiments

These manual, benchmark-only checks preserve the production parser and chunker.
See the [accuracy follow-up](../bench/parsers/reports/2026-09-09-odl-accuracy-followup.md)
for measured scope and source evidence.

| File | About |
| --- | --- |
| [`experiment_prince_native_alt.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_prince_native_alt.py) | `--check` verifies list-item insertion ownership and duplicate/complex-scope abstention. Explicit `--pdf`, `--parsed`, `--output` replays source accessibility alternatives into exact-matching cached blocks, checks page-99 distances and all answer associations, preserves failed/abstained cases, and probes in-memory ActualText extraction. Counts are intervention counts, not whole-book accuracy; no model, service, or source writes. |
| [`experiment_prince_headings.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_prince_headings.py) | `--self-check` and six-book cached replay compare folio-less margin-banner removal, complete outline-root matching, full-outline remapping and rejected front-matter name demotion. Measures breadcrumb ancestry, body coverage, outline-heading retention and changed canonical chunks; no parsing service or embeddings. |
| [`experiment_prince_math_loss.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_prince_math_loss.py) | Frozen source-region detector experiment for vector/raster content missing from native text, with negative controls, synthetic raster probes, raw-chunk reason propagation and actual passage-header visibility. Optional `--whole-book` measures warning volume and runtime. Region detection does not establish formula recall or recovery accuracy. |
| [`fetch_unseen_pdfs.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/fetch_unseen_pdfs.py) | Fetches explicit public-source seeds with a size bound, SHA-256 identity, PDF metadata and failure receipts. Private benchmark downloads stay under ignored `fixtures/local/`; the frozen unseen source manifest records provenance and licensing. |
| [`validate_parser_repairs.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/validate_parser_repairs.py) | Fresh production parsing under a per-document process-group deadline, frozen code/source hashes, heading-change receipts, full-book chunk scoring, and source/rewritten-PDF fidelity checks for the bounded Java retry. Independent source-only heading release gold remains separate from output judgments. |
| [`experiment_coherent_banner_roles.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_coherent_banner_roles.py) / [`audit_coherent_banner_scope.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/audit_coherent_banner_scope.py) | Replays confirmed recurring bands and neutral scope boundaries on cached full-document native output. Checks shared cited-region paths and an independent body-occurrence scope comparison, preserving frozen baseline results and recording captured-module provenance. |
| [`compare_parser_repair_regressions.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_parser_repair_regressions.py) | Compares fresh production BOJ/Chemistry blocks and chunk text/path/page/region signatures with the cached tested heading candidate, excluding confidence annotations absent from the cached run. Rechecks body coverage, outline anchors and intended ancestry changes. |
| [`replay_heading_roles.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/replay_heading_roles.py) | Replays working-tree `correct_roles` and `mark_page_numbers` against a baseline git revision on the same saved outputs and PDFs, with no Java stage. `regression` counts removed banners and folios, bullet demotions, other block changes and body blocks gaining an ancestor; `books` counts wrong-path chunks with the section-path investigation's `measure.py`; `--strict` stops blocks that the saved run already removed from proving a folio offset. |
| [`gate_parser_fresh.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/gate_parser_fresh.py) | Fresh-parse gate over two running parser services: `parse` sends each source to both arms through `/file_parse`, and `compare` packs both with the production chunker. It checks body retention, outline anchors and roots, banners the baseline removed that the candidate keeps, gold witnesses, an ancestry diff over every shared body block (lost outline-confirmed ancestors), and wrong-path chunks for intake books. `deck` builds a numbered-title variant of a real PPTX. |
| [`experiment_outline_scope_guard.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_outline_scope_guard.py) | Checks complete-root promotion against existing heading and neutral-banner boundaries. Unique outline children and split-title continuations preserve legitimate scope; unknown peer boundaries cause abstention. Replays 37 prior/new documents and original Physics, with source-bound payload/path checks on LibreOffice and OECD. |
| [`run_unseen_pdf_parse.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/run_unseen_pdf_parse.py) | Isolated, full-document paired parsing of unseen PDFs using current repository code and a frozen heading candidate at the real heading stage. Freezes source/code identities, verifies identical native Java output, preserves failures/timeouts, and resumes completed attempts. `--self-check` checks manifest validation. |
| [`evaluate_unseen_headings.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/evaluate_unseen_headings.py) | Packs completed baseline/candidate parses with each arm's own furniture, compares source-bound body coverage and outline retention, distinguishes regrouped chunks from text loss, and queues every heading change for source review. Abstentions and unfinished documents remain separate. |
| [`evaluate_unseen_native_alt.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/evaluate_unseen_native_alt.py) | Applies the frozen native-alternative prototype to unseen heading parses, verifies parse receipts and common implementation identities, checks original character/list-item preservation, and retains exact decisions and scored chunks. Intervention counts are not visual accuracy. |
| [`evaluate_unseen_visual_detector.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/evaluate_unseen_visual_detector.py) | Scores the unchanged geometry detector against source-only frozen rectangles across new exporter families, retaining true/false positives, misses and non-binary cases separately. Source hashes and detector identity are checked; detector negatives do not certify extraction correctness. |
| [`collect_unseen_visual_output.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/collect_unseen_visual_output.py) | Joins frozen source witnesses to completed raw parses and scored chunks for manual assessment. Region overlap is evidence selection only; unavailable outputs and cases without an explicit judgment remain unassessed. |
| [`experiment_unseen_ecb_repair.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_unseen_ecb_repair.py) | Post-holdout derived-copy experiment for an ODL hybrid-xref reader failure. Requires explicit initial-save or keep-encryption arm, checks source/parser identities and full-page text/pixel/geometry/outline/link fidelity, and verifies encryption/metadata against eight other source controls. Original holdout failure remains separate; no production normalization. |
| [`probe_office_bookmarks.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/probe_office_bookmarks.py) | Generates synthetic Office fixtures and verifies LibreOffice Writer named bookmark destinations, separately reporting passage-text fidelity. Requires local Office libraries and LibreOffice; no application writes. |
| [`probe_office_pdf.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/probe_office_pdf.py), [`probe_preview_regeneration.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/probe_preview_regeneration.py) | Disposable parser-image probes capture real ODL output and compare regenerated PDF text, every character box and rendered pixels against frozen source hashes. No queues, databases or blob-store calls. |
| [`evaluate_native_citations.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/evaluate_native_citations.py) | Hash-joined exact-text resolution, abstention controls and Google/LibreOffice PDF comparisons. Conditional geometry and target-resolution metrics do not certify end-to-end citation accuracy. See the [native-citation report](../bench/parsers/reports/2026-09-14-native-citations.md). |
| [`odl_structure_audit.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/odl_structure_audit.py) | Read-only inventory of native ODL hierarchy, caption/continuation links, cell geometry and their survival through saved adapted blocks and chunks. Measures metadata retention and shared regions, not parsing accuracy. |
| [`audit_textbook_structure.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/audit_textbook_structure.py) | Read-only local textbook scan of source-matched running banners, visually reviewed figure/table labels in heading paths, and path-only excerpt fragmentation. Hashes source PDFs and saved artifacts; `--self-check` covers six narrow header-selector controls. No parsing, provider calls, database writes or production classification changes. |
| [`experiment_odl_textbook_recovery.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_textbook_recovery.py) | `check`, `run` and `validate` compare saved post-table bundles using occurrence-specific furniture and source-role heading candidates. Frozen three-book and six historical controls check exact baseline replay, literal/region conservation, five missing fragments, five false-parent witnesses, original multilingual heading checks, excerpt membership and citation contracts. Does not validate fresh pre-table parser ordering or exact mathematical reconstruction. |
| [verify_odl_textbook_fix.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/verify_odl_textbook_fix.py) / [score_odl_textbook_fix.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/score_odl_textbook_fix.py) | Full-book local HTTP parsing, production bundle validation/packing/confidence, archived baseline and frozen independent witnesses. Body checks bind to the matching raw page/box; table-header words in prose or cross-page overlap do not count. No embeddings or database/model calls. |
| [compare_odl_spectrum.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_odl_spectrum.py) | Compares explicit saved baseline/current runs with repeatable source manifests, phase timings, historical heading controls, selected raw-field multisets and previously visible non-heading text at its page/box. `--check` verifies page/box isolation, allowed role changes, text changes and duplicate counts. New source-role checks require the accompanying manual adjudication where inherited headings or merged table/caption blocks make generic counts ambiguous. |
| [compare_selective_recovery.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_selective_recovery.py), [compare_selective_recovery_check.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_selective_recovery_check.py), [compare_selective_recovery_review.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_selective_recovery_review.py) | Sixteen frozen visual crops compare CPU MinerU with normal Qwen3.8-Flash, thinking off and strict JSON Schema. Retains historical Batch collection and native request/usage receipts. Offline checks verify identical PNG/Base64/raster-PDF pixels without native text; source review separates exact math, associated cells, complete tables, HTML and neighboring text. Manual selection does not measure automatic routing recall. |
| [compare_deepseek_recovery.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_deepseek_recovery.py) | Bounded DeepSeek V4.1 Flash comparison on frozen Qwen crops, twelve selected tag requests and three summaries. Explicit Responses JSON-object/JSON-Schema arms, thinking off by default with a `--reasoning` option (none, low, high, max) that freezes into its own directory, native receipts including reasoning tokens, and exact evidence checks. Plain-text page/region prompt arms retain the same crop images and add four full-page fixtures. Offline `check` covers message/image conversion, exact prompt placement, plain-text/empty-text validation, schema rejection, no retries, unavailable metrics and preserved replay timing. No production provider change. |
| [compare_claude_recovery.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_claude_recovery.py) | Headless Claude Code arm over the same 16 frozen crops: `claude -p` on the subscription login, Sonnet 4.6 medium, stream-json image input, `--json-schema` structured output, built-in tools and connector MCP servers off, per-crop receipts with both per-call and CLI aggregate usage, no retries. Offline `check` covers request conversion, credential-env stripping, usage deduplication, local schema rejection and replay without retry. Comparison only; no production provider change. |
| [compare_tokenhub_recovery.py](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_tokenhub_recovery.py) | GLM-5.3-Flash arm over the same 16 frozen crops through the production TokenHub chat-completions gateway with `reasoning_effort` low/high/max, JSON-object output checked against the frozen schema locally, native receipts with reasoning and cached token counts, no retries. Offline `check` covers body conversion, reasoning-token accounting, schema rejection and replay without retry. Comparison only. |
| [knowledge_base_library.py](../bench/rag/scripts/knowledge_base_library.py) | Owns the live library: `schema` creates the `LIBRARY_SCHEMA` layout and adds nullable retrieval metadata to existing excerpt tables and the figure `label`, `credit` and `decorative` columns to existing figure tables and `withheld_pages` to existing book tables (written by each publish from the run manifest), `publish --run [--book]` loads each book as its next version under a new content id and swaps its pointer (refusing an unchanged corpus identity, recording `books/<sha256>.pdf` only after a knowledge-base bucket `head_object`), `rollback` and `retire` move and drop retained versions (`retire` keeps the version row and its model runs and drops the topics only that version kept alive), `remove --book` deletes every row of a book across all its versions and the topics only it kept while leaving its bucket PDF, `status` lists books with their history and the subjects holding topics. `schema` and every `publish` load `lab/knowledge/subjects.json`; a publish takes topics from `<run>/topics.json` or the manifest, refuses a book whose subject is not in the fixture and a topic whose id is a subject id, and drops topics no tagged excerpt of a current or retained version references. `rollback`, `retire`, `remove`, the topic drop, the subject sync refusal, per-subject status counts and the section-title summary are also covered live by `pipeline/tests/test_library.py`. Offline `check` covers excerpt/tag joining including failed and missing tag outcomes, capture path joining with and without `captures.json`, repository-relative paths, the section-title summary and its clip, topics from the run or the manifest with a subject mismatch and a subject-id collision refused (the message naming the topic and the subject), book selection from `--book` or the run's corpora, model-run receipts from stage state and the embedding log when `usage-summary.json` is absent, version-scoped ids, corpus identity covering the tag outcome so a retag is a new version and the manifest's `withheld_pages` so withholding or restoring pages is one, and the object-key check refusing a book the bucket does not hold. Run `uv run --project pipeline python bench/rag/scripts/knowledge_base_library.py check` from the repository root; a bare interpreter has no psycopg. |
| [knowledge_review_samples.py](../bench/rag/scripts/knowledge_review_samples.py), [test_knowledge_review_samples.py](../bench/rag/scripts/test_knowledge_review_samples.py) | Text-only Qwen3.8-Max book review trial with seven synthetic cases, a real saved candidate sample and a repaired control. Accepts a fixture `--case` or a saved Sol `--packet`; `--thinking` enables a comparison without changing saved defaults. Explicit `--dry-run` builds requests without credentials or network; `--send` saves one request, result and usage. Offline checks cover strict JSON Schema in both thinking modes with no caller token cap, answer-key isolation, both input modes' no-send boundary, exact target coverage, assigned fields, verbatim evidence and consistent review status. No library writes, automatic retries, images or material review. |
| [`compare_mineru_pipeline_local.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/compare_mineru_pipeline_local.py), [`review_mineru_pipeline_local.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/review_mineru_pipeline_local.py) | Hash-frozen, byte-identical 28-page PDF slices compare current refined ODL with explicit MinerU pipeline, CPU-only, two serialized passes. Preserves parser wall times and native artifacts; the review command runs current packing/heading/confidence code for separate source inspection. Diagnostic cases cover formulas, table values/scopes, false heading roles and an OCR-layer scan. No model APIs or indexing; selected slices do not estimate whole-book accuracy, and MinerU confidence is an uncalibrated compatibility diagnostic. |
| [`structure_chunking_eval.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/structure_chunking_eval.py), [`structure_chunking_embed.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/structure_chunking_embed.py), [`structure_chunking_jlpt_eval.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/structure_chunking_jlpt_eval.py) | Frozen PDF boundary/relationship experiments measuring chunk counts, source anchors, term coverage and gold-page retrieval, with a separate fresh-embedding comparison and replay of the original JLPT query vectors. Native parsing and authorized embedding calls use isolated benchmark resources; page hits and term coverage are not answer-accuracy measurements. |
| [`section_boundary_eval.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/section_boundary_eval.py) | Paired current-pipeline packing and adjacent native list/table boundaries across 13 complete PDFs, with fixed anchor/cleanliness probes and hash-bound embedding reuse. Declares separate eight-source locator and 13-source legacy/JLPT query pools; source-page overlap is a retrieval proxy, not answer accuracy. |
| [`experiment_odl_font_recovery.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_font_recovery.py) | `check` validates literal embedded Type1 encoding, narrow contradictory-map eligibility and explicit glyph-derived cmap output. `prepare/run/score` audit sources, compare fresh Java output and rendered-pixel identity, and check frozen numeric rows and prose identifiers. |
| [`experiment_odl_native_improvements.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_native_improvements.py) | Manual `freeze/parse/improve/audit` experiment with fresh Java output, source-defined table rows, actual chunks, rejected extraction/replacement variants, double-overprint evidence and separate source controls. Reports structural associations separately from flat-text and chunk retention. |
| [`experiment_odl_layout_ocr.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_layout_ocr.py) | Historical: imports `parser/odl/ocr.py`, which the 2026-10-15 VLM change removed, so it no longer runs against the current parser. It was a manual real PP-OCRv6/PP-DocLayoutV3 comparison with frozen source-region order, negative controls, complete line permutations and actual chunk artifacts (`score` needed no models; `table-rows` preserved a rejected development variant). Its diagnostic corpus results never certified unseen-layout accuracy. |
| [`validate_odl_repairs.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/validate_odl_repairs.py) | Source/gold freeze for four new publisher families, explicit raw Java and production-matched baselines, candidate native/fresh-OCR runs, saved-output scoring and final packing. Preserves source/script/model hashes and failed receipts; reports scoped row/cell text and region ordering separately from unscored header semantics. |
| [`verify_odl_runtime.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/verify_odl_runtime.py) | Explicit loopback/container/spool verification of whole-document artifacts, FIFO capacity (executing up to `parse_workers`, the rest queued), hard-timeout and OOM quarantine. Records responses, health/cgroup measurements and bundle entry counts and sizes (v5 bundles carry no images); never starts services or contacts the shared ingest host. |
| [`experiment_odl_reading_order.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_reading_order.py) | `--self-check` validates conservative column ordering, unchanged blocks, source-confirmed rotated headings, same-font sentence continuations including list tails, and preservation of page/region geometry. Selected experiment flags are `--move-rotated --split-continuations`. Requires PyMuPDF 1.28.2. |
| [`experiment_odl_native_tables.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_native_tables.py) | `--check` covers exact native header/title/unit packing, shared body-cell scope, citation coverage, cross-page rejection and explicit ordinary packing for unsupported native tables. Corpus replay compares production, existing structured, strict context and bounded context arms. |
| [`experiment_odl_source_styles.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_source_styles.py) | `--check` distinguishes yellow, white and blue cell backgrounds, rejects later opaque fills and preserves caller data. Corpus replay retains literal source-confirmed yellow cell annotations and records strict structured-packing failures separately. Requires PyMuPDF 1.28.2. |
| [`replay_odl_accuracy.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/replay_odl_accuracy.py) | Composes explicitly selected saved font, reading-order and cell-style outputs before bounded native table packing. Rejects baseline mismatches, case-set differences and overlapping edits; records input/output/source hashes. This is an offline interaction check, not a combined latency benchmark. |
| [`experiment_odl_heading_context.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_heading_context.py) | `--check` builds real PDF spans to verify numeric footer sequences, rotated chapter tabs, repeated banners and metadata conservation. Saved-output replay scores actual chunks, section paths and indexed text against frozen source checks; selected arm `structure` preserves continuing sections across pages. Requires PyMuPDF 1.28.2. |
| [`experiment_odl_ocr_disagreement.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_ocr_disagreement.py) | Positional `check` verifies fragment matching, native column order, protected pages and idempotence. `prepare/ocr/replay` retain source gates and rejected OCR variants. The selected repair reorders native blocks on eligible hidden OCR-layer pages; automatic OCR text replacement was rejected. |
| [`experiment_odl_table_geometry.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_table_geometry.py) | `--check` covers ruled-region selection, row/header geometry, row-label spans and partial-overlap abstention. Ruled-table extraction and optional `--row-label-spans` remain bounded experiments, outside the integrated arms. Replacement rejects partially overlapping or unpositioned native blocks; explicit oracle overrides do not establish a safe repair. Requires PyMuPDF 1.28.2. |
| [`measure_odl_native_pipeline.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/measure_odl_native_pipeline.py) | `root output --variant baseline\|native\|extended\|refined` runs fresh Java parsing, adaptation and one chunk pass with source hashes, snapshots, phase timings and memory sampling. Native adds the four first-pass repairs; extended adds source-backed headings and native hidden-layer column order; refined composes the third-pass source repairs and explicit footer ancestry. Detector/header controls are explicit experiments with unchanged defaults. Run sequentially on an idle VM. Excludes OCR, captions, indexing and application queues. |
| [`score_odl_font_transfer.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/score_odl_font_transfer.py) | `root pdf checks output` scores completed neighboring-paper font runs against frozen numeric sequences and the clean-font control, retaining raw and chunk evidence. Value recovery does not certify row/header associations or table semantics. |
| [`score_odl_native_pipeline.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/score_odl_native_pipeline.py) | `local output` verifies all three measured arms across three repeats and eight documents/254 pages, source/output hashes, content conservation, frozen heading checks, Japanese native table context, prior source-scored chunks and German/NIST continuations. Produces a new verification JSON from completed artifacts. |
| [`experiment_odl_native_text.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_native_text.py) | `--check` and corpus replay validate source-confirmed overprint deletion and paragraph-role repair, exact unedited blocks/geometry, idempotence and frozen actual-chunk checks. The original frozen helper handles text blocks. |
| [`experiment_odl_heading_retention.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_heading_retention.py) | Replays source-visible orphan-heading retention after chunking, preserving every existing chunk and adding literal source text with source boxes; source checks reject OCR footers and already-indexed labels, including terminal hyphens at section/body boundaries. |
| [`experiment_odl_exponents.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_exponents.py) | `--sources MANIFEST --output NEW_DIR` checks source-bound scientific exponents, unchanged geometry and caller fields, caret-only edits and idempotence. Writes actual chunks and per-occurrence source evidence. |
| [`experiment_odl_math_recovery.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_math_recovery.py) | Isolated OCR consensus, layout detection, formula recognition and replay retain fixed source crops, automatic detection, negative controls and actual-chunk formula/prose checks. Recognition runs require the VM model runtimes. |
| [`experiment_odl_table_integration.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_table_integration.py) | `--check` and full-source scans audit extraction separately from automatic replacement, retain all rejected candidates, and verify unchanged blocks/furniture/chunks. Frozen failed candidates remain evidence, not approved parser behavior. |
| [`score_odl_independent.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/score_odl_independent.py) | `--check` rejects changed row values and invented missing values. `--checks FIXTURE --runs RUN_DIR... --output JSON` compares frozen row/prose sequences in native output and actual chunks; complete table meaning and formulas require the separate source rubric. |
| [`experiment_odl_list_text.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_list_text.py) | Reuses the source overprint proof for list items while preserving item and whitespace boundaries; includes legitimate repetition and boundary ambiguity controls. |
| [`experiment_odl_list_geometry.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_list_geometry.py) | Requires exact Java list identity and source binding before unioning same-page text-bearing descendant boxes; checks excluded descendants, geometry-only text conservation, final citations and idempotence. |
| [`experiment_odl_column_continuation.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/experiment_odl_column_continuation.py) | Moves a complete continuation only when source text, column geometry and font evidence distinguish intervening small print; preserves every block and checks actual prose chunks. |
| [`refine_odl_output.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/refine_odl_output.py) | Composes source-bound native repairs before one final chunk pass, preserving rejected candidates and phase evidence. Fresh parsing runs through `measure_odl_native_pipeline.py --variant refined`; explicit alternate detector and header/footer controls preserve existing runner defaults. |
| [`score_odl_refined.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/score_odl_refined.py) | Applies frozen text, list, section-context, retention and NIST-fragment diagnostics to either parser's final chunks, retaining individual verdicts and input hashes. These narrow checks do not certify full-document accuracy. |

[`evaluate_odl_table_geometry.py`](../bench/parsers/scripts/evaluate_odl_table_geometry.py)
in `integrate` mode replays the eight source-reviewed
tables through conservative and explicit manual-override replacement. Its `score`
mode checks complete source grids, actual document chunks, retained blocks and
the disclosed source-rubric correction. It uses the fixed retained experiment
inputs; it does not evaluate every accepted geometry region. The measured
pipeline scorer also accepts `--extended-prefix` to verify equivalent later
source-inspection variants against the original expanded output.

The [second-pass report](../bench/parsers/reports/2026-09-09-odl-accuracy-second-pass.md)
and [font-transfer report](../bench/parsers/reports/2026-09-09-odl-font-transfer.md)
record scope and limitations. The
[parser benchmark README](../bench/parsers/README.md#native-opendataloader-second-pass)
lists executable focused checks, measurement commands and evidence-scoring
commands; scorers require the retained local artifacts and fresh output paths.

- `bench/parsers/scripts/verify_native_viewers.ts`: real Office iframe citation updates, visible overlay painting and clearing without document reload.
- `pipeline/tests/test_parser_app.py`: v6 PDF-free Office bundle, frozen evidence/chunk parity and bounded capture dispatch.
- `pipeline/tests/test_capture.py`: temporary Office source cleanup, JPEG-only capture response, and format routing preserved after PDF/Office renames.

### Frontend workspace and signup flows

- Static validation (2026-09-27): dashboard Recent Items applies the existing `scroll-fade-y` utility to Panel's overflow container with its `py-5` bottom-padding offset; `pnpm run fmt`, `pnpm run fix`, and scoped `git diff --check` passed. No browser visual check was run.
- Manual Chromium CSS check (2026-09-17): shared textarea sizing at 14px/20px and 16px/24px font/line-height fits five lines without scrolling and scrolls at six and ten lines; height stays capped including padding and borders. Isolated CSS check, not a full app test.
- `e2e/editor/frontend-workspaces.spec.ts`: MSW browser checks for workspace sort options naming their default order, the menu closing on a choice, and mouse/keyboard order reversal from the active option, shared create/edit icon and description fields (an over-limit description is kept, marked invalid and blocks Create), automatic close after a successful save with persisted fields on reopen, mobile create layout, signup resend cooldown and failed-send retry, workspace card settings/statistics, Biology PDF/empty file states, standalone invitations, the single ownership-transfer confirmation, and a route chunk answered with HTML showing the update fallback inside the shell's one main landmark. The PDF error assertion allows 30 seconds for viewer initialization. Run `pnpm run e2e:msw:editor --workers=1 frontend-workspaces.spec.ts`.

- Local intake check `data/knowledge-base/opus-intake-2026-09-23/test_withholding.py` (ignored workspace data): exact citation preservation inside restricted-text cuts, malformed/ambiguous/out-of-order fragment rejection, citation-only classification, source-reviewed dependent metadata, verbatim evidence, unrelated-content protection and exact restoration. Run `uv run --project pipeline python data/knowledge-base/opus-intake-2026-09-23/test_withholding.py`; no database writes or model calls.
- `lab/knowledge/tests/test_topics.py`: reviewed-context full excerpt coverage and stable boundaries; complete synopses preserved and old topic IDs excluded; delegated context export/proposal import without model calls, stale review and invalid schema/ID rejection, alongside topic merge and 96-topic subject-ceiling tests. Library-wide topic ids: a proposed new id another subject holds is refused, a `shared` id is merged as that subject's live topic and final tags may use it, and the loader keeps a shared topic's subject, never upserts it and refuses a publish that would move a topic to another subject or share one that is not live (a hand-edited shared entry without its subject included); it looks live subjects up by topic id, and a proposal's `shared` must be a list of ids.
- `lab/knowledge/tests/test_packet.py`: self-contained text packets preserve prior notes separately from candidate tags, require the frozen original-note hash, include required linked source text, reject unknown links/source mismatches, and keep the documented example compatible with the strict non-thinking review request without sending it. `test_enrich.py` also exercises the actual apply-then-export sequence, rejects updated live notes as the baseline and accepts the existing immutable backup.
- `lab/knowledge/tests/test_source_repair.py`: source-bound chunk repairs rebuild excerpt/index text and content hashes, preserve untouched source and full-note baselines, validate evidence against projected text, back up canonical corpus on apply, reject stale corpus/text/page-image bindings and no-op or unrelated patches, embed repaired linked context with correction provenance in scoped packets, require complete repair assessments and no-edit source bindings, and resume interrupted exact-artifact apply plans while refusing unrelated file changes. Section-path repairs move only the locator (chunk, first-chunk excerpt path, indexed text, content hash, `original_section_path` provenance) on a bound page anywhere in the book, combine with a text fix that still needs its chunk's page, and reject no-op paths, unbound pages and changed text; a follow-up artifact applies over an already repaired corpus with its own backup and plan, and under `--stage source-cleanup` leaves the review's `models/retrieval-review` receipt in place; topic context export accepts a validated path correction and refuses the same path unrepaired. `path_form` allows a wholly empty path and rejects empty parts, line breaks and stray separators; `refresh-figures` refuses a book whose chunks carry source repairs and leaves its corpus untouched; a review naming only `requested_model` or `actual_runtime_model` validates and records that name, one naming no model is refused.
- `lab/knowledge/tests/test_figure_notes.py`: `apply_figure_notes` writes a book agent's label, description, credit and decorative flag onto the corpus figure records and changes nothing else; a refresh then puts the notes into the search text without the decorative one while `content_hash` and the excerpts stay; the applier refuses another book's or a stale corpus hash, a missing, repeated or unknown figure id, a wrong page, a wrong type and a missing or extra field with the corpus untouched; `refresh-figures` keeps the four fields by figure id while applying a new figure exclusion; `rebuild_indexed_text` adds no note for decorative figures, keeps the note of an excluded one and uses the recorded label; `knowledge_base_library.corpus_identity` stays byte-identical to the pre-notes digest for a book without notes (no figures, an excluded figure, transcribe's label and description) and, for a noted book, changes with each note field and the excluded flag and still computes after a transcribe redo drops label and description.
- `lab/knowledge/tests/test_qwen_batch.py`: optional advisory batches freeze strict-schema requests and packets without credentials/network or token caps; both thinking modes, model consistency, repeated submission without duplicate jobs, endpoint binding, out-of-order/partial responses for every terminal status, preserved warnings/reasoning, frozen-schema offline recollection, and ten-minute read polling after network or HTTP 429/5xx interruptions while permanent rejections stop. Shared `bench/rag/scripts/test_knowledge_base_pilot.py` also checks uncertain upload/create recovery and refusal to resubmit ambiguous writes.

- `lab/knowledge/tests/test_store.py`: atomic concurrent queue claims and host-delay preservation; bounded SQLite work for large blocked URL/download queues.
- `lab/knowledge/tests/test_server.py`: manual 403 rejection eligibility and a busy rejection returning 503 while other dashboard requests remain responsive.

- `lab/knowledge/tests/test_scrape.py`: English textbooks at all four classified levels pass the level gate; missing levels, disallowed licences and other eligibility failures remain rejected.

- `src/lib/textLength.test.ts`: input counters (`CharCount`, the chat composer, comments) count Unicode code points like the backend's runes, so CJK and emoji count once. Python chat admission overflow is covered by `pipeline/tests/test_retrieval_helpers.py`.
- `server/cmd/openapi/main_test.go`: generated TypeScript and Python field limits match the Go source of truth; catches stale generated constants.

- `bench/rag/scripts/rich_chat_validity.py` and `rich_chat_score.ts`: manual synthetic answer-format sample using the shipped prompt, provider request builders (DeepSeek High, GLM Low), raw-text protocol-leak detection, Python validation, official OpenUI parser and local recovery checks; raw responses and prompt hashes stay in `bench/rag/reports/local/`. Nine checks passed; failed fallback produces one local error notice.

- `e2e/editor/scenario-journeys.spec.ts`: one-click source save failure with the closeable `delayed` save banner (back on the next failure, no toast), cancelled file-link navigation with unsaved changes, and retry without remounting, every recovery path (source replacement and a recovered draft under the `changed` banner, a save refused for good under `refused`) showing the edits read-only and selectable with Reload in the close button's place and no download or discard, across a reload (IndexedDB), until its Reload clears the drafts, a refused DOCX save leaving the edit selectable and copyable (the runtime not inert) across a reload until Reload, a refused PPTX save leaving the slide text copyable until Reload, Office checkpoint failure after editing valid files (the `delayed` banner until Saved), accepted View/Edit URL transitions, Office Edit reloads, and rejected-export URL retention, scenario reset, page/form retries, pending auth cancellation, pending import polling/reset, permanent state reload/reset, Office export failure keeping the iframe, Edit mode and its URL with draft download, note permission loss through the parent guard, Office strips above the count row, one storage status test (amber triangle, dialog and dashboard meter near the limit;
