- `e2e/editor/biology-office.spec.ts`: Biology 101's committed rich DOCX/XLSX/PPTX fixtures open in native View/Edit, save through the header's File › Save (`e2e/helpers/office.ts`), verify real PPTX clipboard paste and Chromium IME composition in the reopened viewer, and retain their source links and Biology workspace identity after User scenarios Reset. A held native DOCX worker edit keeps Saving and the close warning active before and during Save, then reaches Saved after release. The open DOCX viewer keeps its runtime iframe, with no new `source-session` request, when the workspace switches between one and two columns at lg. At 1280x800 with the Files panel open, arrow keys from B4 to H5 on the XLSX editor's `CC info` scroll H5 out from behind the window's edge, and typing `7` with Enter edits it; an edit wheeled out of view keeps its input focused and the next key types into it and scrolls the cell back; clicking the formula bar commits a cell edit and keeps the formula bar focused twice in a row, and a formula-bar edit then survives a grid click; clicking the fx label commits an edit and the arrow keys move the grid again, as they do after clicking a sheet tab. In XLSX view mode, View › Zoom › 200% makes the point twice as far from the grid's corner hit B2 ("Course Name"), going back to 100% halves the scroll (the top-left cell holds), and the formula bar's text still copies with Ctrl/Cmd+C; in edit mode too, 200% back to 100% halves the scroll. With `Faculty Database` saved scrolled to row 400 (routed bytes), the viewer at 50% opens it there after the short `Summary` sheet. In each format, View › Zoom › 150% in view mode (PPTX from 100%, as it opens fitted) scales the first page, the sheet's scroll area or the slide by 1.5 and ticks 150%; switching to Edit shows 150% in the toolbar's zoom control and View › Zoom (DOCX and PPTX at the same page and slide size); back in View the size and tick are still 150% (PPTX: the slide scrolls from its left edge and the Notes button keeps its corner while it scrolls); and in Edit again, once a newer version pauses the editor, the toolbar's zoom control stays enabled and sets 200% (XLSX by typing 300, which Google Sheets takes as 200%). With Capy's chat open, clicking its input commits an open cell edit and reaches Saved, also when the frame's own blur cannot tell (its `hasFocus` stubbed false), through the host's `focus-left`; cell text the engine refuses (over 32,767 characters) shows the editor's own error and no Capy file error; a simulated app switch (both documents without focus, focus kept in the frame) leaves the edit open and the next key types into it. In XLSX View the name box goes to a typed cell: `h5000` scrolls far past the used range, `a1048576` is selected with the scroll area stopping at 15,000,000 px, `'summary'!b3:c4` opens Summary on B3, and text that names no cell stays until Escape restores the address. A DOCX runtime that reloads by itself gets Capy's theme again, and a runtime that starts under another protocol version shows the page-reload prompt. Opening the Office citation of the `OpenUI: Office citation` chat (`chat-openui-office`, a quote from `exchange-plan.docx`) highlights its passage in the DOCX viewer, and the same highlight (size and place) returns after the runtime reloads by itself. A PPTX citation at 200% (`set-citation` for slide 1's `UAT_RUN_MARKER` line) scrolls the viewer's scroller from its top-right until the line is centred; the same citation again, with the line still in view, leaves the scroll where it is, and a repaint after the reader scrolls back to the top-left (the window resizing) does not scroll to it again. A DOCX or XLSX editor whose session is replaced (`announceSourceEpoch` from the mock collaboration module) pauses read-only: File › Save is disabled with Download usable, DOCX's Find and replace takes typing with Replace disabled, Edit › Select all and Ctrl/Cmd+C copy the document or sheet, an IME composition (CDP `imeSetComposition`/`insertText`) into the focused document input or grid, a typed key (no XLSX cell editor opens), Backspace and an editing command posted straight to the runtime send no updates and leave the copy unchanged; the XLSX formula bar's text copies and typing into it changes nothing; the runtime takes each `load` 1.5 s late, so after it reloads by itself `set-capabilities` comes first, and it still opens paused, copies and sends nothing for a typed key. One DOCX focus test opens exchange-plan.docx once (the runtime takes every `load` 5 s late) and runs four steps. A newly opened DOCX editor leaves the focus in the chat box when the user typed there before it finished loading. Switched to View and back, it takes the focus and edits from its first sync while the room's first save receipt is held back (`holdSourceSaveReceipts` in the mock collaboration module), then reaches Saved once it is released, so every typed key has landed before the next step counts updates. Resuming from a pause leaves the focus where it was: Capy's chat box keeps the typing, Find typed into while paused keeps the focus and the next keys search, a new comment and a reply being typed hide while paused and come back with their drafts, and keys typed after the pause reach neither them nor the document (no updates). Switched to View and back again, it leaves the focus in an open Capy menu: the Files panel's Add file menu, opened before it is ready, stays open past `collaboration-ready` and takes Escape. One double click on the header opens header editing, and closing it from its Options menu, or with Escape (the DOCX scenario's lesson.docx, which has a header), gives the document input the focus back. A view-only XLSX viewer that gets `set-capabilities` with `canEdit: false` still selects a cell and copies its contents from the formula bar. PPTX Present puts the slides in full screen in the tab and Esc ends the show; Presenter view from Present's arrow opens a second page with the presenter window (slide 1 of 20, no notes), whose Next advances the tab's show and shows slide 2's notes, and whose End closes it and returns the file at slide 2; a runtime reload closes an open notes window and the next Presenter view gets a fresh one at slide 1. PPTX speaker notes start hidden; in view mode the Notes button and View › Show speaker notes flip one state, at 390px the chat button sits above the open notes box, and edit mode opens with the same choice, which hiding there carries back to view. DOCX rulers start hidden; View › Show ruler shows both, the horizontal one at the first page's left edge and width and the vertical one at its top, at 1280px with the side panel open; view mode draws none and its View menu has no toggle; edit mode opens with them again; after `set-capabilities` with `canEdit: false` (Save disabled) they stay and the toggle still hides them. DOCX view mode copies from its text layer: a drag over the title copies its three paragraphs line by line, a double click copies `2023`, a triple click its paragraph, the selection survives its page scrolling out of the viewport (pages near the top turned plain text, the selected page did not) and back, and Cmd/Ctrl+A copies the whole document with its tables tab-separated and the last page's text. Format › Table on a new 2×2 table: Pin header row ticks, Auto-fit then Center (a radio item) moves the table over 100px right, dragging its right edge afterwards widens only the last column (the drag starts from the drawn widths), and a pause disables Format › Table; a table inserted into exchange-plan.docx (compatibility mode 15) puts its text one cell margin (7.2px) in from the body text. On a phone (360px, touch): a DOCX opens fitted (the 816px page plus 24px padding each side fills the pane), re-fits at 390, 844x390 and 1440 (there 100%), carries the fit into Edit and re-fits there, then View › Zoom › 100% stops the re-fit (390 keeps 816px) and carries back to View; a touch pinch (CDP `synthesizePinchGesture`) zooms the document past 1.5x while Capy's page keeps scale 1, a resize keeps the pinched width, and Edit opens at it and pinches back below 0.75x; at 768x1024 View and Edit open at 816px (100%); in Edit at the fitted zoom (under 40%), a drag over the title copies its three paragraphs, a double click copies `2023`, and a click in the right half of 團 then `Z` makes the paragraph `交流學習團Z活動計劃書`; a chat citation (OpenUI: Office citation, source 1) opens the DOCX fitted with the passage's highlight in view, nothing scrolled sideways, and at the same place on its page as at 100% (768), also after 740x360 and back; a DOCX with a comment (the routed `exchange-plan-comments.docx` and its seeded state) opens in Edit at 390 fitted with the comments sidebar closed and no sideways scroll, and at 1280 with it open; Edit at 390, fitted and at 100%, with the caret on page 1 and page 6 at the pane's top keeps page 6 there through 844x390 and back and a re-fit at 430 (no faces left to load), and a typed key then scrolls to the caret on page 1 (fails with the font relayout as a local edit, or, fitted, without the zoom-change anchor). Insert › Table of contents in the DOCX (after Format › Paragraph styles makes `1. 引言` Heading 1 and `1.1.  活動背景` Heading 2; Update is not offered before) lists both headings with page numbers in front of the caret's paragraph, and after typing `X` at the heading's end Insert › Update table of contents lists `1. 引言X` instead, reaching Saved. At 360px in Edit the chat button's bottom edge sits 8px above the XLSX sheet tabs and the PPTX slide strip, rises with the PPTX notes box and returns when it closes; a `bottom-inset` of 1e9 posted from the runtime frame leaves the button on the page (security regression: office-style REVIEW1 S1). The XLSX toolbar row, its Bold button and icon measure 36/30/18px at 360px and 40/32/18px at 768px (icon-size option B). Run `pnpm run e2e:msw:editor --workers=1 biology-office.spec.ts`.

Popover appearance ownership is documented in [frontend/motion.md](frontend/motion.md). The shared border/background/shadow consolidation adds no new test cases; filter and notification content retain transparent, borderless, shadowless wrappers, and tag autocomplete retains `shadow-lg`.

- `collaboration-rs/crates/server/tests/rooms.rs` (`an_agent_edit_commits_its_row_reaches_the_live_room_and_replays_by_operation`): replace-block lands in the live room and answers after the projection; the revision-checked form compares the draft revision under the material lock and refuses a pending projection.
- `collaboration-rs/crates/server/tests/rooms.rs` (`an_undo_stores_its_redo_and_a_changed_target_refuses_it`): Undo, Redo, Undo each store the next step, and a Redo whose span changed is refused as `stale_target`.
- `pipeline/tests/test_agent.py` (`test_tool_rows_name_their_targets_and_carry_their_run_times`, `test_results_teach_the_rows_their_names`): tool rows show titles, book and chapter, page and slide labels with one lookup for unknown targets, and carry matching `startedAt`/`endedAt` on events and persisted blocks.


### Question bank and shared question format

- `e2e/editor/question-bank-paging.spec.ts`: the bank loads a long mock topic 10 questions at a time, a list click on an unloaded question loads its page plus the next in one request and scrolls it to the panel top, then questions 11–20 load above on their own without moving it; scrolled up to question 11, questions 1–10 wait while a (synthetic) finger is down and go in without moving the reading position once it lifts. Run `pnpm run e2e:slow --config=e2e/editor/playwright.editor.config.ts question-bank-paging.spec.ts`.
- `e2e/editor/question-bank-answering.spec.ts`: in view mode a typed answer is checked, its review shows the part score and accepted answers, the topic list counts it and marks its row correct, and Try again clears the field; the /qb landing shows the latest topic's progress line and Summary for a finished topic, "Continue at question 5" opens the next unanswered question, the Partially wrong status filter narrows the list, and a question picked in the panel after Clone copies into a new quiz named in the quiz picker (typing over the default renames it; after ✕, typing disables Copy until Enter), filed under a chapter typed in the dialog; a finished topic's summary shows its score, the wrong count and a Retry per missed question, and a red square opens its question. Run `pnpm run e2e:slow --config=e2e/editor/playwright.editor.config.ts question-bank-answering.spec.ts`.
- `e2e/editor/question-formula.spec.ts`: real MathLive inside the question Plate editor (opened by an outline row, which focuses the block, then Enter) accepts a physical digit and retains it after commit/reopen, guarding against Slate canceling MathLive input; native menu templates also insert inside the question dialog. Question viewing and editing previews use inert MathLive with matching conjugate/derivative/plain-text dimensions. Block note editing preserves height; its ghost toggle, Escape and outside clicks dismiss the keyboard, keyboard taps still enter digits, and commit cleans it up. Inline question editing omits the keyboard toggle. At 390 px the quiz editor's reaction equation, wider than its column, scrolls sideways in its own box under the wheel while no scroller around it and not the page overflows; its `scroll-fade-x` edges read 0px/28px at rest and 28px/0px scrolled to the end, and reopened at 1440 px, where it fits, its box has no fade and `mask-image: none`. Run `pnpm run e2e:slow --config=e2e/editor/playwright.editor.config.ts question-formula.spec.ts`.
- `server/internal/questions/blank_test.go`: a question counts as blank only when every part is empty (whitespace, empty gaps, no matched pair), not with a chosen option or `false`, and never with an ordering part.
- `server/internal/questions/score_test.go`: the shared scoring fixtures in `server/internal/questions/testdata/scoring/` (mcq sets with one or several correct options, boolean, fuzzy short answers with the JavaScript string rules, exact quantities, whole-order ordering, matching and gaps per item with half marks; also read by the `grade.ts` Vitest), a learner view of malformed stored content, and `Graded` awarding every part without touching the stored question.
- `server/internal/questions/questions_test.go`, `collaboration-rs/crates/notes/tests/validation.rs`, `src/features/questions/validation.test.ts`: shared valid/invalid fixtures for the seven answer types (an mcq with several correct options, `mcq-several-correct.json`; an open part with no sample answer, `open-no-sample.json`, and two refused, `open-two-samples-rejected.json`), bank asset policy, quiz editor-asset images, user-quiz part/marking-item/sample-answer bounds (the bank keeps its own), gap answers (one non-empty accepted list per gap, blanks (1)…(n) in the text matching them), graph references (including angles, arcs, sectors and polygons, repeated points, a centre that is also an endpoint, and segment `ticks` within 1–3)/static SVG, quantity units/zero denominator, Unicode bounds, part marks (required, a marking scheme only on open parts with items that add up to the part's marks, no zero-mark or string items) and attempt-only awards. `questions_test.go` also covers quiz-wide part and open-part caps and the Go grading text (stem, earlier parts, figures, tables, chart data). It also checks that a quiz figure may link only under the bank's asset base (`QuizBankAssetsURL`), and only when one is set; `validation.test.ts` checks the same in the browser under `VITE_BANK_ASSETS_URL`. The Go validator names the part and the rule when a closed part carries a markscheme. A chart with an empty category label is valid in every validator (`chart-description.json`), as are an image without a description (`quiz-image-no-description.json`) and a one-point scatter (`plot-scatter-one-point.json`), while a one-point line is refused (`plot-line-one-point-rejected.json`); a chart with null values (bar and pie, `chart-null-value.json`) is valid and a series short of its labels is refused (`chart-short-series-rejected.json`; the normalisers pad before validating); the Go grading text writes a figure without a description as `[Figure]`. Plot blocks are tested in Go (`TestPlotBlockBoundsAndGradingText`: curves, points, reference lines and marks within bounds, and the grading text listing points and labels) and in `validation.test.ts` with the same cases, outside the shared fixtures, which the removed Node server generated; the Rust validator tests the same cases in `validation.rs` (`a_plot_validates_as_go_validates_it`).
- `src/features/questions/parseMathText.test.ts`, `TextEditor.test.ts`, `graph.test.ts`, `graphSyntax.test.ts`, `src/components/charts/CategoryChart.test.tsx`, `PlotChart.test.tsx`: math-token boundaries and editor serialization, safe graph expressions/export recipe, label wrapping and GeoGebra tick steps and numbers, Desmos-style graph rows to recipe elements and back (scientific numbers as `m·10ⁿ`), shared chart rendering (a null value draws nothing in every chart kind, a line breaking at it), and a plot's clean ticks, x-ordered curve, dashed reference line and labelled points.
- `src/features/notes/blocks/plotFence.test.ts`: a ` ```plot ` fence in agent markdown becomes a valid `plot` node whose fence body writes the same block back, and a plot the block schema refuses fails the conversion.
- `src/features/quizzes/QuizForm.test.ts`, `src/features/materials/document.test.ts`: canonical question round-trips through Plate/JSON/YAML, legacy rejection, note chart fences and part IDs unique across a material.
- `src/features/quizzes/QuestionRunner.test.tsx`: a server-graded review renders its part scores as given, the answer before a collapsed solution, and fixed units; open parts show one Jev mark per marking item and a correct, partial or skipped verdict over their marking points; matching answered by option text on a shuffled learner view offers letter buttons as well as the menu and letters the stored options on review with the chosen and correct letters; an ordering answered by item text keeps the learner's order and lists the correct order with where each misplaced item was put.
- `src/api/anonymous.test.ts`: the signed-out grade route's 429 body (`errors[0].message` `anonymous_grading_limit`) is recognised as the daily grading cap.
- `src/features/study/railMap/map.test.ts`: Learning's railway map is the same for a workspace and different for another, keeps its drawing when items are added (except near the old terminus), never orders two dense biomes together or a city beside open country, fills a panel wider than a short workspace, keeps every desktop view under 120 elements, wraps station names on two lines with an ellipsis, and its stored density labels match `measure()`.
- `src/features/study/finishToasts.test.ts`: the finish toast fires for a topic or workspace finished by a progress refetch (including a topic first listed already finished, or one reopened by new questions and finished again) and not for one finished before.
- `src/features/questions/trailMap/map.test.ts`: the /qb progress map is the same for a topic and different for another, keeps its drawing when questions are added (except the last stretch before the old summit), never orders two dense biomes together, three sparse in a row or more than three without a dense one, keeps the busiest desktop view under 80 elements, and its stored biome density labels match `measure()`.
- `src/features/questions/bankSearch.test.ts`: the /qb panel search returns exams, subjects and topics separately, each matched by its own name and ranked a name's start before a later word, then a match inside (an exam's description counting from the word-start rank), and leaves out an exam's hidden one subject and a single-topic exam's topic.
- `src/features/questions/QuestionView.test.tsx`: the review answer line typesets bare-LaTeX accepted answers and joins a plain `°` unit to each accepted value while other units keep the space; a one-part question shows its marks once, in the header.
- `server/internal/store/listing_test.go`: `TestEmptyQuizListingHasZeroQuestions` covers the empty quiz sentinel.
- `server/internal/review/review_test.go`: answer histories weigh in the order Epo signed off (eight correct answers add nothing, a single first miss leans on "not known yet"); question scores map to Again below 0.5, Hard below 0.7, otherwise Good; a lapsed item ranks as less retained and a miss on a new item counts as a lapse; the question hash covers only the stem and part prompt text, not answers, marking schemes, marks, layout or images; question scores use part marks with half marks.
- `server/internal/bank/bank_test.go`, `server/internal/httpapi/bank_test.go`: migrations, lazy pools, roles, answer stripping outside the editor read, stale saves, persistent review, library and web source attribution, public asset validation/upload and one comment delivery through a test sender.
- `server/cmd/bank/main_test.go`: publication refuses an exam no catalog added, `bank exams` gives a new exam the default cover and keeps a stored cover when the catalog sets none, the committed syllabus catalogs load, insert-only publication retains reviewed edits, starts question numbering at one, and checks exact source shapes: `kind` required, library fields only on library sources, and web sources with a dated retrieval and a licence that is neither ND nor NC.
- `server/internal/store/bank_progress_test.go`, `server/internal/bank/progress_test.go`, `server/internal/httpapi/bank_progress_test.go`: latest-score progress, marks and topic progress with hash resets and Continue's next question, the one-question server check on Check answer, retraction in every bank read, check, list and copy, signed-out refusal, frozen accounts recording but not copying, and account purge.
- `server/cmd/openapi/main_test.go`: Go question limits regenerate matching browser, collaboration and Python constants.
- `pipeline/tests/test_retrieval_helpers.py`: `test_normalize_questions_canonical_ids_and_optional_level` and `test_normalize_questions_rejects_legacy_or_partial_output` cover generated question identity and schema admission, including part marks and marking schemes on open parts only.
- `uv run --project pipeline python lab/questions/run.py check`: blind projection, fixed-unit answers, copy checks, frozen Codex packet binding/immutability and correction of schema-valid wrong-count/wrong-part output before admission; no model or publisher calls.

- `e2e/editor/editor-status.spec.ts`: header cloud icons and keyboard tooltips, initial Synced, immediate Syncing during edits and checkpoint waits, Saved after acknowledgment, older receipts preserving newer pending edits, Offline retained through edits and late acknowledgments, reconnect resending pending checkpoints, and status removal in View mode.

Seeded editor runs use `node_modules/.vite-editor-e2e` for Vite's dependency cache,
separate from a running developer server's cache. Vite prebundles the export
worker's `buffer` and `katex` dependencies so the first Word export cannot trigger
late dependency optimization and reload concurrent browser tests.
Editor benchmark readiness accepts Synced, Syncing and Saved; durability and
save-cycle measurements still wait specifically for Saved.

MSW workspace seed-count check: reload Workspaces; English Literature shows zero chapters and zero source files, while opening it still lists its Gatsby study note. Workspace totals are derived from seeded chapter and source-file records in src/mocks/db.ts.

Biology quiz visual check: with MSW enabled, open `/materials/qz_1?mode=edit` or either Biology note's quiz block. Verify 10 questions, all seven answer types, both layouts, formulas and the surface-area graph; open a question's Worked solution in its editor outline. Open `/quizzes/attempts/at_1` and expand marking schemes/worked solutions to inspect all six chart styles, calculation methods, tables and partial credit. The saved score is 13.5/21. Check desktop and phone widths, and save an edited question to verify the fixture remains valid quiz content.

Client export diagnostic: `node bench/editor/scripts/export-probe-server.mjs`, then `node bench/editor/scripts/export-probe.mjs`. A dedicated production bundle measures Markdown serialization and HTML-to-DOCX generation in the window and a module worker at normal and 4x CPU throttle, using 1,000/9,800 nodes and a 1.96 MB source case. Records elapsed time, interval delay and worker/image compatibility failures under ignored `bench/editor/.results/export-2026-09-27/`. This is a local conversion probe, not an end-to-end export or CI gate.

- `src/features/notes/export/export.test.ts`: readable feature-matrix export with its seeded image caption, seven answer types and multipart solutions, Markdown escaping/nesting, blue DOCX callouts and void dividers, unsupported/missing content, figure deduplication, native TOC placement, repeated/Unicode headings, exclusion of answer labels, and an interactive HTML block exported as an `Interactive snippet` link to the note with `block=<id>` in Markdown and DOCX without calling the figure renderer.
- `e2e/editor/exports.spec.ts`: actual toolbar Markdown (zipped with the seeded image) and DOCX downloads with resolved quiz/card content, native Word video metadata, TOC and page-reference fields, heading/label separation, TOC styles, update-on-open, hyperlinks/bookmarks, compatibility mode, embedded images and selected colors. Run `pnpm e2e:slow --config=e2e/editor/playwright.editor.config.ts e2e/editor/exports.spec.ts`.
- Client export implementation benchmark: set `EXPORT_CLIENT=1`, run `node bench/editor/scripts/export-probe-server.mjs`, then `node bench/editor/scripts/export-client.mjs`. Measures the production worker pipeline through returned blobs at document limits, 1,300 mixed blocks and 100 distinct formulas, including DOM figure rendering with 5,000 mounted paragraphs. Findings: `bench/editor/reports/2026-09-27-client-export-implementation.md`.
- [`e2e/editor/comments.spec.ts`](../e2e/editor/comments.spec.ts): Cursor-only and empty-block comments persist the containing block ID without text anchors; selected-text comments and a native selection made in the same task as the toolbar Comment click or as Ctrl+K (the command palette must store the range Slate has not synced yet) retain their quote, anchors and highlight. Setup verifies the selected text and empty-block deletion preserves the next paragraph; every case exposes the saved thread beside the block.
- `e2e/editor/equations.spec.ts`: Block/inline opening and closing preserve geometry, block keyboard/menu buttons stack vertically while inline formulas have only a vertically centered menu, ATP text and math carets share a baseline, native physical arrow shortcuts work at text boundaries and survive commit while word interiors remain text, and mouse-drag selection keeps one MathLive instance with readable app-theme colors when system and app themes differ. Formula menus insert matrices/templates, switch modes, copy/paste LaTeX, and reopen after outside dismissal or removal of an editor with its menu open without browser errors. Committed matrix/template placeholders render as boxes and reopen as editable slots. A formula committed as `\cssId{capy-probe}{x}` previews the command as text and puts no such id into the page (security regression: read-only formulas draw MathLive's HTML commands as text).
- `e2e/editor/formula-metrics.spec.ts`: Plain formula text retains its view/edit font and width; single/multiple-character conjugates use one arrow step per character in both directions and remain editable, while fraction navigation retains its native stops.
- `bench/editor/scripts/formula-parity.audit.ts` (`pnpm bench:formula`, not in CI; run it on MathLive upgrades, output in `bench/editor/.results/formula-parity/`): Checks all 13 MathLive Insert commands and 25 matrix sizes, empty/filled in inline/block modes (152 cases). Asserts view/edit dimensions and per-glyph/bar geometry within 0.1px, identical fonts/text, valid rendering and LaTeX round trips. Captures every pair and attaches geometry JSON. The original KaTeX comparison and MathLive follow-up are in `artifacts/2026-09-28-formula-parity/`.
- `e2e/editor/formula-opening.spec.ts`: Checks that an inline formula near the line end and its following text retain their positions/dimensions when editing opens, with a working overlay menu. Samples the first eight animation frames across five opening/closing cycles of a long, wrapped inline formula, alternating Enter and blur to commit; no frame may lose the formula text or collapse its width. Reproduces the cached-import opening flash and the empty preview frame on commit.

- `src/theme/ThemeProvider.test.tsx`: Saved Macchiato and Frappé selections render Frappé as dark in both the initial HTML script and ThemeProvider, migrating the old stored name. Run with `pnpm test`.
- Local approved edition rebuild: `uv run --project pipeline python data/knowledge-base/opus-intake-2026-09-23/check_edition_rebuild.py` checks saved-plan hashes, exact inverse corpus/review equality, all 55 scope bindings and 45 carried reviews with 224 repairs and 54 figure notes. Reads the ignored Industrial Instrumentation operation snapshots; no database writes or model calls. Intended before subsequent import changes its canonical corpus.

- Local intake capture privacy: `uv run --project pipeline python data/knowledge-base/opus-intake-2026-09-23/test_capture_privacy.py` checks immutable source/capture proof bindings, missing or changed copy refusal, exact-book routing and derivative-sensitive version identity. Ignored intake data only; no database writes or model calls.

- Local approved licence rebuild: `uv run --project pipeline python data/knowledge-base/opus-intake-2026-09-23/check_licence_rebuild.py` checks the saved Quantitative Research Methods plan, exact inverse corpus/review equality, all five scope bindings and 52 carried reviews with 85 repairs and 15 figure notes. Reads ignored operation snapshots; no database writes or model calls. Run before subsequent intake changes its canonical corpus.

- Local approved blank-edition rebuild: `uv run --project pipeline python data/knowledge-base/opus-intake-2026-09-23/check_chemistry_rebuild.py` checks Chemistry edition2012 insertion, unchanged remaining manifest fields, exact inverse corpus/review equality, all five scope bindings and 38 carried reviews with 124 repairs and 23 figure notes. Reads ignored operation snapshots; run before the subsequent start-page correction or import changes the canonical corpus.

- Local approved source-note follow-up: `uv run --project pipeline python data/knowledge-base/opus-intake-2026-09-23/introduction-to-human-geography-2nd-edition/source-note-correction-proposal/check.py` checks unchanged default note comparison, acceptance of the hash-bound follow-up, rejection of changed hashes and paths outside the book, and the exact single-field correction with the original baseline preserved. Reads ignored snapshots and the staged or applied helper; no canonical writes.
